Now we have four axioms left. The first one we verify is
\top\cdot p\leqq p
for
p\in\mathcal{O}
. We immediately obtain that
\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{\top\cdot p}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}=\mathsf{State}\cdot\mathsf{State}^{*}\cdot{\left\llbracket p\right\rrbracket}_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{OA}}\cdot\mathsf{State}^{*}\subseteq\mathsf{State}^{*}\cdot{\left\llbracket p\right\rrbracket}_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{OA}}\cdot\mathsf{State}^{*}=\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{p}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}\subseteq\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{p}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\downarrow}
.
The axioms
\top\cdot p\leqq p
,
a\cdot\top\leqq a
, and
\top\cdot a\leqq a
for
a\in\mathsf{Act}
are all verified in a similar manner.

In the inductive step we need to check whether the closure rules for congruence have been preserved. We distinguish four cases.

•

If
e=e_{0}+e_{1}
and
f=f_{0}+f_{1}
with
e_{0}\equiv f_{0}
and
e_{1}\equiv f_{1}
, then by induction we know that
\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{e_{0}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\downarrow}=\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{f_{0}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\downarrow}
and
\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{e_{1}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\downarrow}=\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{f_{1}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\downarrow}
.

\displaystyle={(\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{f_{0}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}\cup\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{f_{1}}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}})}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{exch}\cup\mathsf{contr}}}

\displaystyle={\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{f}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{exch}\cup\mathsf{contr}}}

•

The cases for
\cdot
,
\parallel
and
*
are argued similarly.

Next, we need
[
12
, Lemma 3.4]
, which goes as follows.

Lemma B.49
.

Let
C\in\mathsf{PC}
, let
V,W\in\mathsf{Pom}
, and
\mathtt{a}\in\Sigma
.
If
C[U]=V\parallel W
, then there exists a
C^{\prime}\in\mathsf{PC}
such that
C=C^{\prime}\parallel W
and
C^{\prime}[U]=V
, or
C=V\parallel C^{\prime}
and
C^{\prime}[U]=W
.

Analogously to the previous lemma, we can prove the following.

Lemma B.50
.

Let
C\in\mathsf{PC}
, let
V,W\in\mathsf{Pom}
, and
\mathtt{a}\in\Sigma
.
If
C[\mathtt{a}]=V\cdot W
, then there exists a
C^{\prime}\in\mathsf{PC}
such that
C=C^{\prime}\cdot W
and
C^{\prime}[\mathtt{a}]=V
, or
C=V\cdot C^{\prime}
and
C^{\prime}[\mathtt{a}]=W
.

The following properties of
\mathsf{top}
will also be useful.

Lemma B.51
.

Let
L,K\subseteq\mathsf{SP}
.
The following hold:

{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\cup{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}=\left(L\cup K\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\cdot{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}=\left(L\cdot K\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\parallel{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}=\left(L\parallel K\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}{\left({L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\right)}^{*}=\left(L^{*}\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}

Proof B.52
.

The inclusions from left to right are a consequence of
lemma

B.46
.

For the other inclusions, it is useful to note that, since for all
e\leq f\in\mathsf{top}
we have that
{\left\llbracket e\right\rrbracket}
as well as
{\left\llbracket f\right\rrbracket}
are singletons, the language
{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
can be more simply described as the smallest set containing
L
and satifying the rule

\frac{c\in\mathsf{Act}\cup\mathsf{State}\quad\alpha\in\mathsf{State}\quad C\in\mathsf{PC}\quad C[c]\in{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}}{C[\alpha\cdot c]\in{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\\
C[c\cdot\alpha]\in{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\\
}

We now treat the other inclusions on a case-by-case basis.
for the first one, we show more generally that if
{(L_{n})}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
is a family of pomset languages, then
{\Bigl(\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}L_{n}\Bigr)}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\subseteq\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}{L_{n}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
We do this by induction on the construction of
U\in{\Bigl(\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}L_{n}\Bigr)}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
as induced by the characterisation of
\left(-\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
above.
In the base,
U\in\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}L_{n}
, in which case the claim follows immediately.
Otherwise, if there exist
c\in\mathsf{State}\cup\mathsf{Act}
,
\alpha\in\mathsf{State}
and
C\in\mathsf{PC}
such that
C[c]\in{\Bigl(\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}L_{n}\Bigr)}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
and either
U=C[\alpha\cdot c]
or
U=C[c\cdot\alpha]
, then by induction we know that
C[c]\in{L_{n}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
.
If
U=C[\alpha\cdot c]
, then
U=C[\alpha\cdot c]\in{L_{n}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\subseteq\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}{L_{n}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
The case where
U=C[c\cdot\alpha]
can be treated similarly.

Next, we show that if
U\in\left(L\cdot K\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
, then
U\in{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\cdot{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
In the base,
U\in L\cdot K\subseteq{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\cdot{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
Otherwise, if there exist
c\in\mathsf{State}\cup\mathsf{Act}
,
\alpha\in\mathsf{State}
and
C\in\mathsf{PC}
such that
C[c]\in\left(L\cdot K\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
and either
U=C[\alpha\cdot c]
or
U=C[c\cdot\alpha]
, then by induction we have
C[c]\in{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\cdot{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
, i.e.,
C[c]=V\cdot W
such that
V\in{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
and
W\in{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
By
lemma

B.50
, we find
C^{\prime}\in\mathsf{PC}
with either
C=C^{\prime}\cdot W
and
C^{\prime}[c]=V
, or
C=V\cdot C^{\prime}
and
C^{\prime}[c]=W
.
If
U=C[\alpha\cdot c]
, then in the former case
U=C^{\prime}[\alpha\cdot c]\cdot W\in{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\cdot{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
; in the latter case,
U=V\cdot C^{\prime}[\alpha\cdot c]\in{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\cdot{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
The case where
U=C[c\cdot\alpha]
can be treated similarly.

An argument similar to the case for sequential composition shows that if
\left(L\parallel K\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
is contained in
{L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\parallel{K}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
, where this time we use
lemma

B.49
.

The final containment can be showed using the arguments above; after all, we have

\left(L^{*}\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}={\Bigl(\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}L^{n}\Bigr)}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\subseteq\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\left(L^{n}\right){\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\subseteq\bigcup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\left({L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\right)}^{n}={\left({L}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}\right)}^{*}

Lemma B.53
.

For all
e\in\mathcal{T}
, we have that

\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{e}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{e}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.

Proof B.54
.

By
lemma

B.51
, it suffices to prove the claim for all
a\in\mathsf{Act}\cup\mathcal{O}
; by definition of closure, we already know that
\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}\subseteq{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
For the converse inclusion, we proceed by induction on the construction of
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
as characterised in the previous proof.
Thus, in the base we have
U\in{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
because
U\in\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}
, in which case the claim holds vacuously.
For the inductive step, we have
U\in{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
because there exist
c\in\mathsf{State}\cup\mathsf{Act}
,
\alpha\in\mathsf{State}
, and
C\in\mathsf{PC}
such that
C[c]\in{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
and either
U=C[\alpha\cdot c]
or
U=C[c\cdot\alpha]
.
By induction, we find that
C[c]\in\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}
, and hence
C[c]=V\cdot W\cdot X
for
V,X\in\mathsf{State}^{*}
and
W\in{\left\llbracket a\right\rrbracket}_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{OA}}
or
W=a
, depending on whether
a\in\mathcal{O}
or
a\in\mathsf{Act}
respectively.
By applying
lemma

B.50
twice, we find that either
C=C^{\prime}\cdot W\cdot X
with
C^{\prime}[c]=V
, or
C=V\cdot C^{\prime}\cdot X
with
C^{\prime}[c]=W
, or
C=V\cdot W\cdot C^{\prime}
with
C^{\prime}[c]=X
.
In the first and the last case, we find that
C^{\prime}[\alpha\cdot c],C^{\prime}[c\cdot\alpha]\in\mathsf{State}^{*}
as well, and hence
C[\alpha\cdot c],C[c\cdot\alpha]\in\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}
.
In the second case, we note that

for

(again, depending on whether

Proof B.55
.

We proceed by induction on
e
.
In the base,
e=a
for some
a\in\mathsf{Act}\cup\mathcal{O}
.

First, let
x\in\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}
; in that case,
x=u\cdot y\cdot v
for

and

.
We claim that
u\cdot y
is an element of
{{\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
We proceed by induction on the length of
u
.
If
u=1
, then there are two cases to consider.

•

If
a\in\mathsf{Act}
, then
y=a=\hat{r}(a)
; in that case, we find that
y\in{\left\llbracket y\right\rrbracket}={\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}

•

If
a\in\mathcal{O}

y\in\mathsf{State}
and
y\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{OA}}a
.
In that case
y\in{\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}
as well.

Thus, we have
u\cdot y=y\in{\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}\subseteq{{\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
If
u
has length
m+1
, we know that
u=u^{\prime}\cdot\alpha
for some
\alpha\in\mathsf{State}
and
u^{\prime}
has length
m
.
Our induction hypothesis tells us that

We can take

to obtain from the induction hypothesis that
\{C[y]\}=C[{\left\llbracket y\right\rrbracket}]\subseteq{{\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.
As
\alpha\cdot y\leq y\in\mathsf{top}
, we obtain that
C[{\left\llbracket\alpha\cdot y\right\rrbracket}]\subseteq{{\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
from the definition of closure.
Because

, we have reached the desired conclusion.
Then we can show in a similar matter that
u\cdot y\cdot v\in{{\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
.

For the other direction, we start by proving that

.
We have two cases.

•

a\in\mathsf{Act}
, then
{\left\llbracket\hat{r}(a)\right\rrbracket}=\{a\}\subseteq\mathsf{State}^{*}\cdot\{a\}\cdot\mathsf{State}^{*}=\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}

4

From

\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{a}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}

Proof B.56
.

In the following, let
g=\sum_{\alpha\leq\top}\alpha
.
We start by proving
e\equiv_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}\hat{s}(e)
.
We know already that for
a\in\mathsf{Act}\cup\mathsf{State}
, we have
a\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}g^{*}\cdot a\cdot g^{*}=\hat{s}(a)
, since
1\leqq g^{*}
.

For the other direction, it suffices to show that
\hat{s}(a)\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}a
for
a\in\mathsf{State}\cup\mathsf{Act}
.
To this end, let
a\in\mathsf{State}\cup\mathsf{Act}
.
We are going to use the least fixpoint axioms of KA.
We know that
g\cdot a\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}a
and
a\cdot g\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}a
follow immediately from the hypotheses in
\mathsf{top}
and distributivity.
Hence, we have that
a+a\cdot g\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}a
.
Now we can apply one of the least fixpoint axioms (
e+(f\cdot h)\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}f\Rightarrow e\cdot h^{*}\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}f
) to obtain
a\cdot g^{*}\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}a
.
From this it follows that
a\cdot g^{*}+g\cdot a\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}a
.
To this we apply the other least-fixpoint axiom (
e+(f\cdot h)\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}h\Rightarrow ef^{*}\cdot e\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}h
), to conclude that
g^{*}\cdot a\cdot g^{*}\leqq_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}a
.

The next thing to prove is that
{{\left\llbracket e\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}={\left\llbracket\hat{s}(e)\right\rrbracket}
.
This can be done by noting that
\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\llparenthesis$}}}}}\displaystyle\makebox[0.0pt][c]{\color[rgb]{1,1,1} $\beta$}{e}\mathchoice{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\displaystyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{491520}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.0pt}{$\textstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{344064}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}{\raisebox{-1.94444pt}{\resizebox{245760}{582542}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\rrparenthesis$}}}}}={\left\llbracket\hat{s}(e)\right\rrbracket}
, and hence
{\left\llbracket\hat{s}(e)\right\rrbracket}={{\left\llbracket\hat{s}(e)\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}
by
lemma

B.53
.
From
1
and
e\equiv_{\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{BKA}}^{\mathsf{top}}\hat{s}(e)
we then conclude that
{{\left\llbracket e\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}={{\left\llbracket\hat{s}(e)\right\rrbracket}}{\downarrow^{\mathsf{top}}}={\left\llbracket\hat{s}(e)\right\rrbracket}
.
